podintroductions.100 = {
#Delaying the clan introduction so that chargen created characters are properly handled
	scope = none
	hidden = yes
	
	trigger = {
		}
	
	immediate = {
		#Tremere clan system intro

		every_player = {
			limit = {
				is_tremere_trigger = yes
				POD_is_tremere_main_faith_trigger = yes
			}
		trigger_event = {
				id = pod_tremere_council.6
				days = 11
		}
		}

		##Ventrue ephorate system intro

		if = {
			limit = {
				game_start_date <= 1500.1.3
				}
		every_player = {
			limit = {
				is_ventrue_trigger = yes
			}
		trigger_event = {
				id = pod_ventrue_ephorate.6
				days = 11
		}
		}
		}
		#Giovanni intro

		every_player = {
			limit = {
				is_giovanni_trigger = yes
			}
		trigger_event = {
				id = podgiovanni.1
				days = 11
		}
		}

		#Lasombra intro

		every_player = {
			limit = {
				is_lasombra_trigger = yes
			}
		trigger_event = {
				id = pod_lasombraevents.12
				days = 11
		}
		}

		#Assamite intro

		every_player = {
			limit = {
				is_assamite_trigger = yes
				religion = religion:roadofblood_religion
			}
		trigger_event = {
				id = POD_web_of_knives.7
				days = 30 #Happens later than others, since the WoK takes time before filling itself
		}
		}

		#Nosferatu intro

		every_player = {
			limit = {
				is_nosferatu_trigger = yes
			}
		trigger_event = {
				id = podnosferatu.1
				days = 30 #Happens later than others, since it doesn't matter until later anyway
		}
		}

		#setite intro

		every_player = {
			limit = {
				religion = religion:roadofset_religion
			}
		trigger_event = {
				id = podsetite.1
				days = 30 #Happens later than others, since it doesn't matter until later anyway
		}
		}

		#pope intro

		every_player = {
			limit = {
				faith = faith:shadowinquisition
			}
		trigger_event = {
				id = PODask_pope.1
				days = 30
			}
		}

		#Fera Beastborn adoption intro
		every_player = {
			limit = { has_trait = fera }
			if = {
				limit = { has_faith = faith:bubasti }
				trigger_event = { id = POD_fera_beastborn.2 days = 11 }
			}
			else = { trigger_event = { id = POD_fera_beastborn.1 days = 11 } }
		}
	}
}

podintroductions.1 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = podintroductions.1.t
	desc = podintroductions.1.desc
	theme = claim_throne_scheme
	left_portrait = {
		character = character:505072
		animation = schadenfreude
		}
	right_portrait = {
		character = character:505073
		animation = worry
		}
	lower_center_portrait = {
		character = character:790138
		animation = personality_honorable
		}
	
	trigger = {
		current_date <= 1230.1.15
		current_date >= 1230.1.1
		}
		
	immediate = {
		play_music_cue = "mx_cue_ck3_vocal_maintheme"
		
		}
	
	option = {
		name = podintroductions.1.a
	}
}

			limit = {

podintroductions.2000 = {
	hidden = yes
	
	immediate = {
		# Default introductions
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_trait = supehunter
				has_faith = faith:shadowinquisition
			}
			trigger_event = podintroductions.2102
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_trait = supehunter
				NOT = { has_faith = faith:shadowinquisition }
			}
			trigger_event = podintroductions.2152
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_trait = vampire
			}
			trigger_event = podintroductions.2002
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_trait = kueijin
			}
			trigger_event = podintroductions.5102
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_trait = mummy
			}
			trigger_event = podintroductions.2202
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_trait = fera }
			if = {
				limit = { has_trait = werewolf }
				trigger_event = podintroductionsfera.100
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_trait = bastet }
				trigger_event = podintroductionsfera.200
			}
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_trait = wraith
			}
			trigger_event = podintroductions.2302
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_trait = demon
			}
			trigger_event = podintroductions.2402
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_trait = fae
			}
			trigger_event = podintroductions.2502
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_trait = changeling
			}
			trigger_event = podintroductions.2502
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				POD_is_any_kind_of_ghoul_trigger = yes
			}
			trigger_event = podintroductions.2002
		}
		
		else = {
			trigger_event = POD_character_initialization.1
		}
	}
}

podintroductions.2001 = {
#Called from game start on action with no delay
	hidden = yes
	scope = none
		
	trigger = {
		#current_date <= 1230.1.15
		#current_date >= 1230.1.1
	}
		
	immediate = {
		#Setup for Karl to disappear if not played
		if = {
			limit = {
				character:792915 = {
					is_ai = yes
					is_alive = yes
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					character:792915 = { is_landed = yes }
				}
				character:792915 = {
					create_title_and_vassal_change = {
						type = faction_demand
						save_scope_as = change
					}
					title:c_brixen = {
						change_title_holder = {
							holder = character:790487
							change = scope:change
							take_baronies = yes
						}
					}
					resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:change
				}
			}
			character:792915 = { death = { death_reason = death_disappearance } }
		}

		#Remove the unnamed if not played, needs her narrative to make sense and we might use her elsewhere
		if = {
			limit = {
				character:793085 = {
					is_ai = yes
					is_alive = yes
				}
			}
			character:793085 = {
			destroy_title = title:d_order_dreadful_night
			}
			character:793085 = { death = { death_reason = death_disappearance } }
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = { has_game_rule = pod_shattered_world_counties }
			every_ruler = {
				limit = {
					is_landed = yes
					highest_held_title_tier > tier_county
				}
				every_held_title = {
					limit = { tier > tier_county }
					holder = { destroy_title = prev }
				}
			}
		}
		
		#WW holdings become tribal
		if = {
			limit = {
				game_start_date >= 1230.1.5
				game_start_date <= 1235.1.1
			}
			every_ruler = {
				limit = {
					is_landed = yes
					OR = {
						has_trait = kinfolk
						has_trait = werewolf
					}
				}
				trigger_event = POD_maintenance.17
			}
		}
		
		every_player = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_from_ruler_designer = yes
				}
				# Choose if want be standalone or part of family
				trigger_event = {
					id = podintroductions.6000
					days = 3
				}

				# Add age for immortal
				trigger_event = {
					id = podintroductions.6010
					days = 2
				}

				# Add nickname
				trigger_event = {
					id = POD_custom_nickname.10
					days = 4
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_trait = supehunter
						has_main_supernatural_race_trigger = yes
						NOT = { faith = faith:sanbenito }
					}
					trigger_event = podintroductions.6001
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { has_trait = use_ingame_chargen }

					remove_trait = use_ingame_chargen
					add_character_flag = POD_used_character_generator
					
					POD_remove_all_supernatural_atribute_effect = yes

					remove_character_flag = immune_to_disease
					remove_character_flag = had_POD_character_initialization

					save_scope_as = character_being_changed
					
					house = { save_scope_as = coa_target }
					trigger_event = POD_chargen.2

					# trigger_event = {
					# 	id = podintroductions.2000
					# 	days = 1
					# }
				}
				else = {
					trigger_event = podintroductions.2000
				}
			}
			else = {
				trigger_event = podintroductions.2000
			}

			if = {
				limit = { POD_is_roguelike_mode_enabled_trigger = yes }
				trigger_event = podintroductions.7000
			}
		}
	}
}

podintroductions.2002 = {
	type = character_event
	window = fullscreen_event
	title = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = {
					is_vampire_trigger = no
				}
				desc = podintroductions.2001.t_ghouls
			}
			desc = podintroductions.2001.t
		}
	}
	desc = podintroductions.2001.desc
	theme = vampire
	override_background = { reference = vampire_intro_background }
	override_sound = { reference = "event:/DLC/FP2/SFX/UI/fp2_struggle_ui_intro_end_animate" }
		
	trigger = {
		#current_date <= 1230.1.15
		#current_date >= 1230.1.1
		}
		
	immediate = {
		play_music_cue = "mood_kingdom_of_darkness"
		# if = {
			# limit = {
				# has_game_rule = NPC_vampire_rulers_learn_lifestyle_perks
			# }
			# set_global_variable = {
				# name = POD_learning_starts_now
				# value = yes
			# }
		# }
		POD_achiev_nomnom_initialize = yes
	}
	
	option = {
		
	name = {
		trigger = {
			is_vampire_trigger = no
			}
				text = podintroductions.2001.a_ghouls
			}
	name = {
			text = podintroductions.2001.a
		}
	trigger_event = podintroductions.2003
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			is_vampire_trigger = yes
			}
			
		name = podintroductions.2001.b
		
		trigger_event = podintroductions.2004
	}
	option = {
		name = podintroductions.2001.c
		
		if = {
			limit = {
			 game_start_date = 1510.1.3
			 }
		trigger_event = podintroductions.2006
		}
		else = {
			trigger_event = podintroductions.2005
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = podintroductions.2001.e
		trigger = { years_from_game_start < 1 }
		
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.1
	}

	option = {
		name = podintroductions.2001.f
		
		
		trigger_event = {
			id = podintroductions.3000
			days = 1
		}
			
		custom_tooltip = continue_intro_tooltip
	}
	
	option = {
		name = podintroductions.2001.d
		
		custom_tooltip = end_intro_tooltip
	}
		
}

# Wraith
podintroductions.2302 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = podintroductions.2302.t
	desc = podintroductions.2302.desc
	theme = wraith

	option = {
		name = podintroductions.2302.a
		
		trigger_event = podintroductions.2303
	}

	option = {
		name = podintroductions.2302.b
		
		trigger_event = podintroductions.2304
	}

	option = {
		name = podintroductions.2302.c
		
		trigger_event = podintroductions.2305
	}
	
	option = {
		name = podintroductions.2001.e
		trigger = { years_from_game_start < 1 }
		
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.1
	}

	option = {
		name = podintroductions.2001.f
		
		custom_tooltip = continue_intro_tooltip
		
		trigger_event = {
			id = podintroductions.2310
			days = 1
		}
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.1900
	}
	
	option = {
		name = podintroductions.2001.d
		
		custom_tooltip = end_intro_tooltip
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.1900
	}
}

# Special "the world and its lore" intro for Kuei Jin
podintroductions.5102 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = podintroductions.5102.t
	desc = podintroductions.5102.desc
	theme = vampire
	
	widget = {
		gui = "POD_shader_event_fog_alley_night"
		container = "background_shader_vfx_container"
	}
		
	trigger = {
	}
		
	immediate = {
		play_music_cue = "mood_cathay_in_red"
	}
	
	option = {
	name = podintroductions.5102.a
	trigger_event = podintroductions.5103
	}
	option = {
		name = podintroductions.2001.c
		
		trigger_event = podintroductions.5104
	}
	option = {
		name = podintroductions.2502.c
			
		trigger_event = podintroductions.5105
	}
	
	option = {
		name = podintroductions.2001.e
		trigger = { years_from_game_start < 1 }
		
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.1
	}

	#option = {
	#	name = podintroductions.2001.f
	#
	#	trigger_event = {
	#		id = podintroductions.5000
	#		days = 1
	#	}
	#
	#	custom_tooltip = continue_intro_tooltip
	#	if = {
	#		limit = { has_trait = kueijin }
	#		trigger_event = POD_chargen.1600
	#	}
	#}
	
	option = {
		name = podintroductions.2001.d
		
		custom_tooltip = end_intro_tooltip
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = kueijin }
			trigger_event = POD_chargen.1600
		}
	}

}

podintroductions.3000 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = podintroductions.3000.t
	desc = podintroductions.3000.desc
	theme = vampire
	
	widget = {
		gui = "POD_shader_event_fog_alley_night"
		container = "background_shader_vfx_container"
	}
		
	trigger = {
		}
		
	immediate = {
	}
	
	option = {
		name = podintroductions.3000.a
		
		trigger_event = podintroductions.3001
	}
	option = {
		name = podintroductions.3000.b
		
		trigger = {
				is_vampire_trigger = yes
		}
		
		trigger_event = podintroductions.3002
	}
	option = {
		name = podintroductions.3000.c
		
		trigger_event = podintroductions.3003
	}
	
	option = {
		name = podintroductions.3000.d
		
		trigger_event = podintroductions.3004
	}
	
	option = {
		name = podintroductions.3000.e
		
	}
		
}

#Detailed Mummy intro

podintroductions.2210 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = podintroductions.2210.t
	desc = podintroductions.2210.desc
	theme = mummy
	
	option =  {
		name = podintroductions.2210.a
		
		trigger_event = podintroductions.2211
	}
	option = {
		name = {
			trigger = { has_trait = wutian }
			text = podintroductions.2210.b_wutian
		}
		name = {
			text = podintroductions.2210.b
		}
		
		trigger_event = podintroductions.2212
	}
	option =  {
		name = podintroductions.2210.c
		trigger = { is_amenti_trigger = yes }
		
		trigger_event = podintroductions.2213
	}
	option =  {
		name = podintroductions.2210.e
		
	}
}

podintroductions.2211 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = podintroductions.2211.t
	desc = podintroductions.2211.desc
	theme = mummy
	
	option =  {
		name = podintroductions.2211.a
		
		trigger_event = podintroductions.2210
	}
}

podintroductions.2212 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	theme = mummy
	title = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = {
					has_trait = wutian
				}
				desc = podintroductions.2212.t_wutian
			}

#################
# post RD event #

podintroductions.6000 = {
	hidden = yes
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_trait = demon
					has_trait = mummy
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_trait = vampire
					has_trait = kueijin
				}
			}
			trigger_event = podintroductions.6002
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_trait = supehunter
			}
			trigger_event = podintroductions.6005
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				POD_is_any_kind_of_ghoul_trigger = yes
				NOT = { has_trait = fera }
			}
			trigger_event = podintroductions.6004
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_trait = wraith
			}
			trigger_event = podintroductions.6007
		}
		else = {
			trigger_event = podintroductions.6006
		}
	}
}

podintroductions.6001 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = podintroductions.6001.t
	desc = podintroductions.6001.desc
	theme = martial
	
	option = {
		name = podintroductions.6001.a
		trigger = { current_year > 1658 }
		show_as_unavailable = { current_year <= 1658 }
		
		set_character_faith = faith:sanbenito
	}
	
	option = {
		name = podintroductions.6001.b
		
		remove_trait = supehunter
	}
	
	option = {
		name = podintroductions.6001.c
		
		POD_remove_splat_traits_effect = yes
		add_trait = supehunter
	}
	
	after = {
		if = {
			limit = { POD_has_wrong_religion_trigger = yes }
			POD_adopt_correct_religion_effect = yes
		}
		POD_adopt_correct_government_effect = yes
		POD_remove_wrong_supernatural_traits_effect = yes
		trigger_event = podintroductions.2000
	}
}

podintroductions.6002 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = podintroductions.6002.t
	desc = podintroductions.6002.desc
	theme = vampire
	
	widget = {
		gui = "POD_shader_event_fog_alley_night"
		container = "background_shader_vfx_container"
	}
		
	trigger = {
		POD_is_vampire_trigger = yes
		NOT = { has_character_flag = POD_used_character_generator }
	}
	
	option = {
		name = podintroductions.6002.a
		trigger = {
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = podintroductions.6002.option.a.unavailable
				NOT = { has_character_flag = POD_used_character_generator }
			}
		}
		show_as_unavailable = { has_character_flag = POD_used_character_generator }
		custom_tooltip = podintroductions.6002.option.a.t
		
		
		add_character_flag = chargen_choosing_sire_flag
	}
	
	option = {
		name = podintroductions.6002.b
		custom_tooltip = podintroductions.6002.option.b.t
		trigger = { has_trait = vampire }
		
		add_character_flag = chargen_changing_splat_flag
	}
	
	option = {
		name = podintroductions.6002.c
	}
}

	desc = podintroductions.6007.desc
	theme = family

		name = podintroductions.6007.a
		
		create_character = {
			age = 1
			trait = mortal
			random_traits = yes
			gender_female_chance = 50
			dynasty = none
			faith = root.faith
			culture = root.culture
			location = root.location
			after_creation = {
				copy_inheritable_appearance_from = root
				set_age = { 1 12 }
				POD_copy_base_skills_effect = {
					CLONED_CHAR = root
					CLONE = this
				}
				POD_set_parent_effect = { PARENT = root }
				add_character_flag = had_POD_character_initialization
			}
		}
		add_character_flag = had_mortal_lineage
	}
	option = {
		name = podintroductions.6007.c
	}
}

			container = "background_shader_vfx_container"
		}

		}

		name = podintroductions.6010.a
	}

# Roguelike mode
podintroductions.7000 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = podintroductions.7000.t
	theme = vampire
	
	widget = {
		gui = "POD_shader_event_fog_alley_night"
		container = "background_shader_vfx_container"
	}
	
	desc  = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { POD_is_roguelike_randomization_enabled_trigger = yes }
				desc = podintroductions.7000.desc.randomizer
			}
			desc = podintroductions.7000.desc
		}
	}
	
	# Switch to an existing character (immortals only)
	option = {
		trigger = { POD_is_roguelike_randomization_enabled_trigger = no }
		name = podintroductions.7000.a
		set_global_variable = {
			name  = roguelike_immortality_filter
			value = yes
		}
		POD_select_roguelike_character_effect = yes
	}
	
	# Switch to an existing character (including mortals)
	option = {
		trigger = { POD_is_roguelike_randomization_enabled_trigger = no }
		name = podintroductions.7000.b
		POD_select_roguelike_character_effect = yes
	}
	
	# Curse your current character
	option = {
		trigger = { POD_is_roguelike_randomization_enabled_trigger = no }
		name = podintroductions.7000.c
		trigger_event = podintroductions.7001
	}
	
	# Randomization option
	option = {
		trigger = { POD_is_roguelike_randomization_enabled_trigger = yes }
		name = podintroductions.7000.d
		POD_roguelike_switch_to_randomized_vampire_effect = yes
	}
}

# Roguelike mode: Choose your debuff
podintroductions.7001 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = podintroductions.7001.t
	desc  = podintroductions.7001.desc
	theme = vampire

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = fear
	}
	
	widget = {
		gui = "POD_shader_event_fog_alley_night"
		container = "background_shader_vfx_container"
	}
	
	option = {
		name = podintroductions.7001.a
		random_list = {
			1 = { add_character_modifier = POD_roguelike_no_wealth     }
			1 = { add_character_modifier = POD_roguelike_no_love       }
			1 = { add_character_modifier = POD_roguelike_no_rest       }
			1 = { add_character_modifier = POD_roguelike_no_belongings }
			1 = { add_character_modifier = POD_roguelike_no_curiosity  }
			1 = { add_character_modifier = POD_roguelike_no_guile      }
			1 = { add_character_modifier = POD_roguelike_no_demands    }
		}
		add_character_flag = POD_roguelike_cursed
		custom_tooltip = podintroductions.7001.tt
	}
	
	option = {
		name = podintroductions.7001.c
		add_character_modifier = POD_roguelike_no_wealth
		add_character_flag = POD_roguelike_cursed
		custom_tooltip = podintroductions.7001.tt
	}
	
	option = {
		name = podintroductions.7001.d
		add_character_modifier = POD_roguelike_no_love
		add_character_flag = POD_roguelike_cursed
		custom_tooltip = podintroductions.7001.tt
	}
	
	option = {
		name = podintroductions.7001.e
		add_character_modifier = POD_roguelike_no_rest
		add_character_flag = POD_roguelike_cursed
		custom_tooltip = podintroductions.7001.tt
	}
	
	option = {
		name = podintroductions.7001.f
		add_character_modifier = POD_roguelike_no_belongings
		add_character_flag = POD_roguelike_cursed
		custom_tooltip = podintroductions.7001.tt
	}
	
	option = {
		name = podintroductions.7001.g
		add_character_modifier = POD_roguelike_no_curiosity
		add_character_flag = POD_roguelike_cursed
		custom_tooltip = podintroductions.7001.tt
	}
	
	option = {
		name = podintroductions.7001.h
		add_character_modifier = POD_roguelike_no_guile
		add_character_flag = POD_roguelike_cursed
		custom_tooltip = podintroductions.7001.tt
	}
	
	option = {
		name = podintroductions.7001.i
		add_character_modifier = POD_roguelike_no_demands
		add_character_flag = POD_roguelike_cursed
		custom_tooltip = podintroductions.7001.tt
	}
	
	option = {
		name = podintroductions.7001.b
		add_character_modifier = POD_roguelike_no_wealth
		add_character_modifier = POD_roguelike_no_love
		add_character_modifier = POD_roguelike_no_rest
		add_character_modifier = POD_roguelike_no_belongings
		add_character_modifier = POD_roguelike_no_curiosity
		add_character_modifier = POD_roguelike_no_guile
		add_character_modifier = POD_roguelike_no_demands
		add_character_flag = POD_roguelike_all_curses
		custom_tooltip = podintroductions.7001.tt
	}
	
	option = {
		name = podintroductions.7001.nocurses
		custom_tooltip = podintroductions.7001.nocurses.tt
	}
}

podintroductions.110 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = podintroductions.110.t
	desc = podintroductions.110.desc
	theme = brujah_coa
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = personality_rational
		}
		
	right_portrait = {
		character = character:790004
		animation = scheme
		}

	lower_center_portrait = {
		character = character:790380
		animation = personality_honorable
		}
		
	trigger = {
		current_date <= 1230.1.30
		current_date >= 1230.1.1
		character:790004 = {
			is_alive = yes
			is_ai = yes
			}
		character:790380 = {
			is_alive = yes
			is_ai = yes
			}
		}
		
	immediate = {
		}
	
	option = {
		name = podintroductions.110.a
		
		custom_tooltip = podintroductions.natalya
		
		create_alliance = {
			target = character:790004
			allied_through_owner = character:790104
			allied_through_target = character:790004
			}
		
		character:790004 = { # This opinion modifier controls the interaction, and is removed when breaking the alliance in any way (though on_actions)
			add_opinion = {
				modifier = perk_negotiated_alliance_opinion
				target = character:790104
			}
		}
	
	}
	
	option = {
		name = podintroductions.110.b
		
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				character:790380 = {
					is_ruler = yes
					}
				}
				character:790380 = {
					depose = yes
				}
			}
		}
		
		add_courtier = character:790380
	
	}
		
}

podintroductions.119 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = podintroductions.119.t
	desc = podintroductions.119.desc
	theme = ventrue_coa
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = personality_bold
		}
		
	right_portrait = {
		character = character:790302
		animation = flirtation
		}
	
	lower_center_portrait = {
		character = character:505082
		animation = personality_vengeful
		}
		
	trigger = {
		current_date <= 1230.1.30
		current_date >= 1230.1.1

		character:790302 = {
			is_alive = yes
			is_ai = yes
			}
		}
		
	immediate = {
		}
	
	option = {
		name = podintroductions.119.a
	
	custom_tooltip = jurgen_love_tooltip

	hidden_effect = {
	
	if = {
		limit = {
			character:790302 = {
				is_married = yes
				}
		}
		character:790302 = {
			primary_spouse = {
				save_scope_as = rosamundhusband
				}
		}
		}
		
	if = {
		limit = {
			character:790302 = {
				is_married = yes
				}
		}
		character:790302 = { divorce = scope:rosamundhusband }
		}
		
	if = {
		limit = {
			character:505086 = {
				is_married = yes
				}
		}
		character:505086 = {
			primary_spouse = {
				save_scope_as = jurgenwife
				}
		}
		}
		
	if = {
		limit = {
			character:505086 = {
				is_married = yes
				}
		}
		character:505086 = { divorce = scope:jurgenwife }
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				character:790302 = {
					is_ruler = yes
					}
				}
				character:790302 = {
					depose = yes
				}
			}
		
		}
		
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	
	title:c_mansfeld = {
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = character:505086
			change = scope:change
			take_baronies = yes
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:change
	
	add_courtier = character:790302
		
	marry = character:790302

	set_relation_nemesis = { reason = nemesis_historical target = character:505082	 }
	}
	
	option = {
		name = podintroductions.119.b
		
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = alexander_help_modifier
			years = 20
			}

		set_relation_rival = { reason = rival_historical target = character:790302 }
				
	}

	option = {
		name = podintroductions.119.c
		
		trigger = {
			has_ep3_dlc_trigger = yes
		}

		add_internal_flag = dangerous
		
		custom_tooltip = pod_generic_landless_tooltip

		custom_tooltip = jurgen_landless_tt

		POD_transfer_all_titles_to_character = {
			CHARACTER = character:790151
		}
		
		create_landless_adventurer_title_effect = {
			REASON = flag:voluntary
			FLAVOR_CHAR = root
		}

		scope:new_landless_adventurer.primary_title = {
			set_title_name = d_knights_black_cross
			set_coa = POD_landless_d_knights_black_cross_COA
		}
		
		POD_set_military_order_precept = {
			PRECEPT = flag:specific
		}
		
		add_courtier = character:793097
		add_courtier = character:791304
		
		add_character_modifier = ashen_grandmaster_shadow_1
		add_character_modifier = ashen_grandmaster_disciplined_1
	}
}

podintroductions.1250 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = podintroductions.125.t
	desc = podintroductions.1250.desc
	theme = lasombra_coa
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = personality_bold
		}
		
	right_portrait = {
		character = character:505154
		animation = personality_zealous
		}
		
	trigger = {
		current_date <= 1230.1.30
		current_date >= 1230.1.1
		}
		
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
		character:790323 = {

		# Transfer landed titles
		every_held_title = {
			limit = {
				is_landless_type_title = no
				tier > tier_barony
			}
			add_to_list = target_titles2
		}
		create_title_and_vassal_change = {
			type = granted
			save_scope_as = change2
			add_claim_on_loss = no
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			every_in_list = {
				list = target_titles2
				change_title_holder_include_vassals = {
					holder = character:791254
					change = scope:change2
				}
			}
			resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:change2
		}
		}
		}
		
		hidden_effect = {
		character:790387 = {

		# Transfer landed titles
		every_held_title = {
			limit = {
				is_landless_type_title = no
				tier > tier_barony
			}
			add_to_list = target_titles3
		}
		create_title_and_vassal_change = {
			type = granted
			save_scope_as = change3
			add_claim_on_loss = no
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			every_in_list = {
				list = target_titles3
				change_title_holder_include_vassals = {
					holder = character:791254
					change = scope:change3
				}
			}
			resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:change3
		}
		
		add_opinion = {
			target = root
			opinion = 100
			modifier = grateful_opinion
			}
		}
		}

		character = scope:Adelaida
		animation = personality_content
		}

		current_date >= 1230.1.1
		}

		create_character = {

		create_character = {
			name = "Gerberga"

		create_character = {

			give_nickname = nick_crafty_maiden
			}
		}

	if = {
		limit = {
			character:791708 = {
				is_married = yes
				}
		}
		character:791708 = {
			primary_spouse = {
				save_scope_as = leopoldwife
				}
		}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = podintroductions.213.a
		
	add_prestige = -100
		
	if = {
		limit = {
			character:791708 = {
				is_married = yes
				}
		}

podintroductions.242 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = podintroductions.242.t
	desc = podintroductions.242.desc
	theme = demon
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = schadenfreude
		}
	right_portrait = {
		character = character:791778
		animation = personality_greedy
		}
	artifact = {
		target = scope:vassagos_reliquary
		position = lower_left_portrait
	}
		
	trigger = {
		current_date <= 1230.1.30
		current_date >= 1230.1.1
		character:791778 = {
			is_alive = yes
			is_ai = yes
			}
		}
		
	immediate = {
		random_character_artifact = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = vassagos_reliquary
				}
		save_scope_as = vassagos_reliquary
		}
		character:791778 = {
		random_character_artifact = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = vassago_earring
				}
		save_scope_as = vassago_earring
		}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = podintroductions.242.a
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				character:791778 = {
					is_ruler = yes
					}
				}
				character:791778 = {
					depose = yes
				}
			}
		
		add_courtier = character:791778
		
		character:791778 = {
			set_character_faith = faith:envy
			add_trait = vengeful
		}
		
		destroy_artifact = scope:vassago_earring
	}
	
	option = {
		name = podintroductions.242.b

		scope:vassago_earring = {
			set_owner = root
			add_artifact_modifier = artifact_monthly_lifestyle_xp_2_modifier
		}

		character:791778 = {
			death = {
				death_reason = death_sacrificed_to_gods
			}
		}
	}
}

podintroductions.266 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = podintroductions.248.t
	desc = podintroductions.266.desc
	theme = malkavian_coa
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = personality_callous
		}
	right_portrait = {
		character = character:699999
		animation = personality_honorable
		}
	
	trigger = {
		current_date <= 1230.1.30
		current_date >= 1230.1.1
	}
		
	immediate = {
	}
	
	option = {
		name = podintroductions.266.a

		create_title_and_vassal_change = {
			type = swear_fealty
			save_scope_as = change
			add_claim_on_loss = no
		}
		
		root = {
			change_liege = {
				liege = character:505050
				change = scope:change
				}
			}
		resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:change

		add_prestige = -150
		set_character_faith = faith:viatyrannus
	}
	
	option = {
		name = podintroductions.266.b

		add_gold = 300
		
		spawn_army = {
			uses_supply = no
			inheritable = no
			name = event_troop_default_name
			#levies = {
			#	value = 100
			#}
			men_at_arms = {
				type = walker
				stacks = 6
			}
			
			location = capital_province
		}

		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = indebted_giovanni_modifier
			years = 10
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = podintroductions.266.c

		set_relation_friend = { reason = friend_generic_history target = character:790101 }
		set_character_faith = faith:viapeccati
		create_artifact_iulus_armor_effect = { OWNER = root }
		create_artifact_iulus_sword_effect = { OWNER = root }
	}

	option = {
		name = podintroductions.266.d

		add_prestige = 150
	}
}

		character = root
		animation = personality_honorable
		}

		animation = personality_zealous
		}

		current_date <= 1230.1.30
		current_date >= 1230.1.1
	}

	}

		name = podintroductions.287.a

		custom_tooltip = POD_alliance_formed_tt

		create_alliance = {
			target = character:792968
			allied_through_owner = character:792937
			allied_through_target = character:792968
			}
		
		character:792968 = { # This opinion modifier controls the interaction, and is removed when breaking the alliance in any way (though on_actions)
			add_opinion = {
				modifier = perk_negotiated_alliance_opinion
				target = character:792937
			}
		}

	character:792968 = {
		spawn_army = {
			uses_supply = yes
			inheritable = no
			name = event_troop_default_name
			#levies = {
			#	value = 500
			#}
			men_at_arms = {
				type = durga_chosen
				stacks = 7
			}
			location = capital_province
		}
	}

		character:792968 = {
			add_legitimacy = 200
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				opinion = 50
				modifier = grateful_opinion
				}
			}
	}

	option = {
		name = podintroductions.287.b
		
		add_learning_lifestyle_xp = 1500
		add_breedgifts_lifestyle_xp = 1500
	}
}
